    SELECT TOP(1) [c].[Id], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[Deposit], [c].[ElectricUnitPrice], [c].[EndDate], [c].[MonthlyRent], [c].[NumberOfOccupants], [c].[RoomId], [c].[StartDate], [c].[Status], [c].[TenantId], [c].[WaterUnitPrice], [r].[Id] AS [Id0], [r].[Amenities], [r].[Area], [r].[BuildingId], [r].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [r].[Description], [r].[ImageUrl], [r].[Price], [r].[RoomNumber], [r].[RoomType], [r].[ServiceFee], [r].[Status] AS [Status0], [b].[Id] AS [Id1], [b].[Address], [b].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt1], [b].[Description] AS [Description0], [b].[Name], [b].[OwnerId], [u].[Id] AS [Id2], [u].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt2], [u].[Email], [u].[FullName], [u].[Password], [u].[PhoneNumber], [u].[RefreshToken], [u].[RefreshTokenExpiryTime], [u].[Role], [t].[Id] AS [Id3], [t].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt3], [t].[Email] AS [Email0], [t].[EmergencyContact], [t].[FullName] AS [FullName0], [t].[IdCardNumber], [t].[Phone], [t].[UserId], [u0].[Id] AS [Id4], [u0].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt4], [u0].[Email] AS [Email1], [u0].[FullName] AS [FullName1], [u0].[Password] AS [Password0], [u0].[PhoneNumber] AS [PhoneNumber0], [u0].[RefreshToken] AS [RefreshToken0], [u0].[RefreshTokenExpiryTime] AS [RefreshTokenExpiryTime0], [u0].[Role] AS [Role0]
    FROM [Contracts] AS [c]
    INNER JOIN [Rooms] AS [r] ON [c].[RoomId] = [r].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [Buildings] AS [b] ON [r].[BuildingId] = [b].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [b].[OwnerId] = [u].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [Tenants] AS [t] ON [c].[TenantId] = [t].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [Users] AS [u0] ON [t].[UserId] = [u0].[Id]
    WHERE [c].[RoomId] = @roomId AND [c].[Status] = N'DangHieuLuc'
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [Occupants] AS [o] ON [s].[Id] = [o].[ContractId]
ORDER BY [s].[Id], [s].[Id0], [s].[Id1], [s].[Id2], [s].[Id3], [s].[Id4]
